# Traceability Matrix

**Version:** 1.7
**Date:** 2026-08-21
**Author(s):** John Anderson

This document provides the bi-directional traceability between
the four layers of the `elc` requirements/design/verification
stack:

1. [Software Design Document](SDD.md) (SDD) — architecture and
   module design.
2. [High-Level Requirements](HLRs.md) (HLR) — externally
   observable behaviour the application must provide.
3. [Low-Level Requirements](LLRs.md) (LLR) — per-function
   behavioural contracts that implement the HLRs.
4. [Software Test Plan](STP.md) and the test artefacts
   under [test/](../test/) — Criterion unit tests and Bats
   integration tests that verify each LLR.

It is generated by `tools/render_doc.py` from the
`<traces>` blocks attached to every HLR, LLR, and test in
[doc/Project.xml](Project.xml). The current snapshot covers
**135 HLRs**, **296 LLRs**,
and **357 tests** across
**24 test files**. Every reference
below is a clickable link to the artifact in its source document.

---

## 2. HLR → LLRs → Tests

The primary forward matrix. For each HLR the table lists every
LLR that implements it and every test that exercises that HLR
either directly or through one of its LLRs. The full per-test
catalogue is in [STP.md §3](STP.md#3-test-catalogue).

| HLR | Name | LLRs | Tests |
| --- | ---- | ---- | ----- |
| [`HLR-071`](HLRs.md#HLR-071) | Multiple Target Arguments | [`LLR-CLI-01`](LLRs.md#LLR-CLI-01), [`LLR-DSC-03`](LLRs.md#LLR-DSC-03), [`LLR-SDG-05`](LLRs.md#LLR-SDG-05) | [`a single target is accepted`](STP.md#a single target is accepted), [`files and directories may be intermixed on one command line`](STP.md#files and directories may be intermixed on one command line), [`files_and_directories_are_classified_independently`](STP.md#files_and_directories_are_classified_independently), [`HLR-071: several targets combine into one report`](STP.md#HLR-071: several targets combine into one report), [`missing_target_is_a_usage_error`](STP.md#missing_target_is_a_usage_error), [`several targets are accepted, files and directories intermixed`](STP.md#several targets are accepted, files and directories intermixed), [`several_targets_are_collected_in_order`](STP.md#several_targets_are_collected_in_order), [`single_target_is_collected`](STP.md#single_target_is_collected) |

## 6. Coverage Gaps

The following items have no direct verification. They are
typically structural, configuration, or aspirational
requirements that are verified implicitly by the build system,
by code review, or by the test harness existing at all (rather
than by a dedicated test).

## 7. Conventions

*   Each HLR carries a `*Trace:*` line in [HLRs.md](HLRs.md)
    listing the SDD sections it implements.
*   Each LLR carries a `*Trace:*` line in [LLRs.md](LLRs.md)
    listing the HLRs it implements (with the HLR's short name).
*   Each test function under [test/](../test/) carries a
    doc-comment block citing the LLR(s) and HLR(s) it verifies,
    mirrored into [Project.xml](Project.xml) as `<traces>`.
*   The [Software Test Plan](STP.md) is the canonical
    catalogue of test purposes; this matrix is the canonical
    cross-reference.
*   This matrix is a snapshot. After modifying any of the four
    upstream documents or the test sources, regenerate it via
    `python3 tools/render_doc.py tools/templates/Traceability.md.j2 Traceability --out doc/Traceability.md`.
